				mux IMUX_MUXA[3] = IMUX_A[3] | IMUX_B[3] | IMUX_C[3] | IMUX_D[3];
				mux IMUX_MUXA[4] = IMUX_A[4] | IMUX_B[4] | IMUX_C[4] | IMUX_D[4];
				mux IMUX_MUXA[5] = IMUX_A[5] | IMUX_B[5] | IMUX_C[5] | IMUX_D[5];
				mux IMUX_MUXA[6] = IMUX_A[6] | IMUX_B[6] | IMUX_C[6] | IMUX_D[6];
				mux IMUX_MUXA[7] = IMUX_A[7] | IMUX_B[7] | IMUX_C[7] | IMUX_D[7];
				mux IMUX_MUXB[0] = IMUX_A[0] | IMUX_B[0] | IMUX_C[0] | IMUX_D[0];
				mux IMUX_MUXB[1] = IMUX_A[1] | IMUX_B[1] | IMUX_C[1] | IMUX_D[1];
				mux IMUX_MUXB[2] = IMUX_A[2] | IMUX_B[2] | IMUX_C[2] | IMUX_D[2];
				mux IMUX_MUXB[3] = IMUX_A[3] | IMUX_B[3] | IMUX_C[3] | IMUX_D[3];
				mux IMUX_MUXB[4] = IMUX_A[4] | IMUX_B[4] | IMUX_C[4] | IMUX_D[4];
				mux IMUX_MUXB[5] = IMUX_A[5] | IMUX_B[5] | IMUX_C[5] | IMUX_D[5];
				mux IMUX_MUXB[6] = IMUX_A[6] | IMUX_B[6] | IMUX_C[6] | IMUX_D[6];
				mux IMUX_MUXB[7] = IMUX_A[7] | IMUX_B[7] | IMUX_C[7] | IMUX_D[7];
				mux IMUX_MUXC[0] = IMUX_A[0] | IMUX_B[0] | IMUX_C[0] | IMUX_D[0];
				mux IMUX_MUXC[1] = IMUX_A[1] | IMUX_B[1] | IMUX_C[1] | IMUX_D[1];
				mux IMUX_MUXC[2] = IMUX_A[2] | IMUX_B[2] | IMUX_C[2] | IMUX_D[2];
				mux IMUX_MUXC[3] = IMUX_A[3] | IMUX_B[3] | IMUX_C[3] | IMUX_D[3];
				mux IMUX_MUXC[4] = IMUX_A[4] | IMUX_B[4] | IMUX_C[4] | IMUX_D[4];
				mux IMUX_MUXC[5] = IMUX_A[5] | IMUX_B[5] | IMUX_C[5] | IMUX_D[5];
				mux IMUX_MUXC[6] = IMUX_A[6] | IMUX_B[6] | IMUX_C[6] | IMUX_D[6];
				mux IMUX_MUXC[7] = IMUX_A[7] | IMUX_B[7] | IMUX_C[7] | IMUX_D[7];

				mux IMUX_MUXD[3] = IMUX_A[3] | IMUX_B[3] | IMUX_C[3] | IMUX_D[3];
				mux IMUX_MUXD[4] = IMUX_A[4] | IMUX_B[4] | IMUX_C[4] | IMUX_D[4];
				mux IMUX_MUXD[5] = IMUX_A[5] | IMUX_B[5] | IMUX_C[5] | IMUX_D[5];
				mux IMUX_MUXD[6] = IMUX_A[6] | IMUX_B[6] | IMUX_C[6] | IMUX_D[6];
				mux IMUX_MUXD[7] = IMUX_A[7] | IMUX_B[7] | IMUX_C[7] | IMUX_D[7];
				mux IMUX_M[0] = TIE1 | X0_S[0] | X0_S[1] | X0_N[0] | X0_N[1] | X1_E1[1] | X2_W1[6] | X2_W2[6] | X2_E1[6] | X2_E2[6];
				mux IMUX_M[1] = TIE1 | X0_W[0] | X0_W[1] | X0_E[0] | X0_E[1] | X1_V[0] | X2_W1[0] | X2_W2[0] | X2_E1[0] | X2_E2[0];
				mux IMUX_M[2] = TIE1 | X0_S[0] | X0_S[1] | X0_N[0] | X0_N[1] | X1_V[1] | X2_W1[6] | X2_W2[6] | X2_E1[6] | X2_E2[6];
				mux IMUX_M[3] = TIE1 | X0_W[0] | X0_W[1] | X0_E[0] | X0_E[1] | X1_V[3] | X2_W1[2] | X2_W2[2] | X2_E1[2] | X2_E2[2];
				mux IMUX_M[4] = TIE1 | X0_S[0] | X0_S[1] | X0_N[0] | X0_N[1] | X1_H[2] | X2_W1[4] | X2_W2[4] | X2_E1[4] | X2_E2[4];
				mux IMUX_M[5] = TIE1 | X0_W[0] | X0_W[1] | X0_E[0] | X0_E[1] | X1_V[2] | X2_W1[0] | X2_W2[0] | X2_E1[0] | X2_E2[0];
				mux IMUX_M[6] = TIE1 | X0_S[0] | X0_S[1] | X0_N[0] | X0_N[1] | X1_H[3] | X2_W1[4] | X2_W2[4] | X2_E1[4] | X2_E2[4];
				mux IMUX_M[7] = TIE1 | X0_W[0] | X0_W[1] | X0_E[0] | X0_E[1] | X1_W1[0] | X2_W1[2] | X2_W2[2] | X2_E1[2] | X2_E2[2];
				mux IMUX_CLK[0] = TIE1 | X0_S[0] | X0_S[1] | X0_N[0] | X0_N[1] | X1_S1[0] | X1_S1[1] | X1_S1[2] | X1_S1[3] | X1_N1[0] | X1_N1[1] | X1_N1[2] | X1_N1[3] | X2_S1[6] | X2_S2[6] | X2_N1[6] | X2_N2[6] | PCLK[0] | PCLK[1] | PCLK[2] | PCLK[3] | PCLK[4] | PCLK[5] | PCLK[6] | PCLK[7];
				mux IMUX_CLK[1] = TIE1 | X0_S[0] | X0_S[1] | X0_N[0] | X0_N[1] | X1_S1[0] | X1_S1[1] | X1_S1[2] | X1_S1[3] | X1_N1[0] | X1_N1[1] | X1_N1[2] | X1_N1[3] | X2_S1[6] | X2_S2[6] | X2_N1[6] | X2_N2[6] | PCLK[0] | PCLK[1] | PCLK[2] | PCLK[3] | PCLK[4] | PCLK[5] | PCLK[6] | PCLK[7];
				mux IMUX_LSR[0] = TIE1 | X0_W[0] | X0_W[1] | X0_E[0] | X0_E[1] | X2_W1[3] | X2_W2[3] | X2_E1[3] | X2_E2[3] | X2_S1[3] | X2_S2[3] | X2_N1[3] | X2_N2[3] | PCLK[0] | PCLK[1] | PCLK[2] | PCLK[3] | PCLK[4] | PCLK[5] | PCLK[6] | PCLK[7];

				mux IMUX_CE[0] = TIE1 | X0_W[1] | X0_E[0] | X0_S[1] | X0_N[0] | X2_W1[7] | X2_W2[7] | X2_E1[7] | X2_E2[7] | X2_S1[7] | X2_S2[7] | X2_N1[7] | X2_N2[7] | PCLK[0] | PCLK[1] | PCLK[2] | PCLK[3] | PCLK[4] | PCLK[5] | PCLK[6] | PCLK[7];

			}
		}
